<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req466</div>
</td>
<td>WRESTLING GRASPS VERSUS OTHER ACTIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It should be more difficult or impossible to use the grasps you are using for wrestling in other tasks at the same time.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req467</div>
</td>
<td>UNDEAD HEALING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Undead and construct-type creatures tend to heal when they shouldn't.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req468</div>
</td>
<td>EXTRACT MATERIALS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The extract system needs to be overhauled to support more robust material definitions and more potential extracts from a given creature or plant.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req469</div>
</td>
<td>HUMAN MIGRANT ISSUES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>In the limited test I did of human migrants in a dwarf fortress, there were several issues, including the humans being treated like tame beasts.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req470</div>
</td>
<td>UNDEAD POPULATIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Undead populations aren't tracked properly.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req471</div>
</td>
<td>ASKING DIRECTIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to ask directions more robustly in adventure mode. They should be able to give you directions and location descriptions. You should be able to ask about general or specific items or trades, and various other things. Different creatures might have a broader knowledge of more areas depending on various factors.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req472</div>
</td>
<td>MIXING LIQUID CONTAMINANTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Liquid contaminants should mix so that you can't just act on one of them, assuming they overlap on the object in question.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req473</div>
</td>
<td>MATERIAL REWRITE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There's a technical rewrite required to support things like object materials which are, for instance, the blood of a specific creature.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req474</div>
</td>
<td>PATH BUFFER CLEARING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There's a bit of an optimization that can be done to the periodic path buffer clearing.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req475</div>
</td>
<td>SIEGE ENGINES AND THE Z AXIS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Siege engines need to respect the z axis.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req476</div>
</td>
<td>DEEP OBJECT PLACEMENT ISSUE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There's an issue with object placement in one of the deep areas.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req477</div>
</td>
<td>CONNECTED SITE COMPONENTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Sites in areas with large elevation changes need to connect up their components. For instance, a town built into several areas on a cliff might have some stairs carved into the walls to connect the components.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req478</div>
</td>
<td>LOCAL ARMY MOVEMENT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are some issue with army movement in adventure mode, especially related to armies you are visiting that would like to move.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req479</div>
</td>
<td>GOBLIN TOWER ISSUES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are some issues with swamps, vegetation and subterranean areas in goblin towers.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req480</div>
</td>
<td>DWARVES ON FIRE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Dwarves should really handle themselves a bit better when they are around fire. They should start by recognizing that it's actually a problem to be on fire.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req481</div>
</td>
<td>HATCH OPENING FOR DIAGONAL FLIERS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It needs to be more specific about how fliers moving diagonally and up interact with multiple hatches.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req482</div>
</td>
<td>FISH CLEANING IMPROVEMENTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to remove relevant portions (shell/bones) of fish during cleaning.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req483</div>
</td>
<td>BLOOD VISIBILITY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Blood of all types should be visible on whatever color stone it falls on.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req484</div>
</td>
<td>WIND
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Local wind patterns need to be more interesting.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req485</div>
</td>
<td>GROUNDED/SWIMMING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Need to square the commonly-used notion of being on the ground with swimming.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req486</div>
</td>
<td>VISION AND SWIMMING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Need to work out what should be visible when you are swimming, especially if you are breathing up at the surface.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req487</div>
</td>
<td>WATER PRESERVATION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are some cases where water is deleted when it might be pushed away to other tiles instead.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req488</div>
</td>
<td>WARM STONE PROBLEM
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are some rare cases where warm stone next to lava gets its temperature reset.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req489</div>
</td>
<td>MIGRANT CHILDREN AGES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The ages of migrant children are not based on the raws.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req490</div>
</td>
<td>MATERIAL DEMANDS AND MANDATES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Local materials lists for demands aren't compiled properly now.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req491</div>
</td>
<td>ADVENTURE MODE TRADE SKILLS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Some of the features from dwarf mode trade can be moved over to adventure mode.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req492</div>
</td>
<td>MELTED OBJECT SIZE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>When objects change states, their sizes need to be tracked more carefully.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req493</div>
</td>
<td>MISSING MIGRANT TYPES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>A few professions don't occur as migrant types.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req494</div>
</td>
<td>INITIALIZING PREFERENCES ISSUE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There's a minor issue with unit preferences and which ones it knows about at which times.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req495</div>
</td>
<td>FISH SEARCH
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Fisherdwarves don't make the same efforts to find locations as thirsty dwarves.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req496</div>
</td>
<td>WORLD MIGRANT LINKS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Migrants joining towns on the world map don't get proper entity links.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req497</div>
</td>
<td>RIVER STONES FOR ENTITIES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Civilizations don't use alluvial deposits properly.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req498</div>
</td>
<td>SAME-SQUARE MOVEMENT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are some adventure mode issues with same-square interactions.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req499</div>
</td>
<td>BETTER PRESSURE
</td>
<td>Completed
</td>
<td>Water should avoid upward paths when there is a good downward route, though this taxes the CPU slightly.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req500</div>
</td>
<td>SITE POPULATIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are various little issues with site animal populations that need to be cleaned up.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req501</div>
</td>
<td>MINOR WILDERNESS POP ISSUES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are some minor issues with the distributions of local wilderness populations.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req502</div>
</td>
<td>FROZEN WATER CONTAMINANTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Contaminants for frozen liquids aren't tracked carefully enough.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req503</div>
</td>
<td>FLIERS/SWIMMERS AND BUILDING TARGETING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Creatures with unusual movement types can't target buildings properly.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req504</div>
</td>
<td>RUIN PLACEMENT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Ruin placement is disabled in sites that become crowded with buildings, but it should adapt instead.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req505</div>
</td>
<td>CAVE-IN DUST
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Dust flows from cave-ins need to be generated more carefully.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req506</div>
</td>
<td>IMPROVED CAVE-IN MECHANICS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The cave-in framework isn't being used for much. There should be more requirements for supports again.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req507</div>
</td>
<td>SOIL CAVE-INS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Certain rock/ground types shouldn't support anything by themselves.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req508</div>
</td>
<td>WALKING ON BUILDINGS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It seems like you should be able to walk over the top of something like a floodgate on the tile above, but this is quite annoying to handle.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req509</div>
</td>
<td>CASTRATING ANIMALS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Dwarves should be able to castrate male animals. Spaying female animals might be beyond the ability of dwarves to do reliably.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req510</div>
</td>
<td>FIRES IN CONTAINERS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Fires should not be able to burn in sealed containers.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req511</div>
</td>
<td>ARMORED AGAINST JUSTICE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>People shouldn't be able to throw armor on a dwarf that's about to be hammered. All such items should be removed before the sentence is carried out.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req512</div>
</td>
<td>PERSONAL HYGIENE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Dwarves get filthy with all manner of foul matter and should clean themselves. Soap might come up. Clothes should also be cleaned. Could have stains.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req513</div>
</td>
<td>FOOD PLURALS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Food items need plurals (for the description screen for example).
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req514</div>
</td>
<td>MAP GENERATION BIAS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The mid-level square maps never use the bottom right corner if all biome priorities are the same.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req515</div>
</td>
<td>CONSTRUCTION BUILDING PLACEMENT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You shouldn't have to place construction squares one by one, and you shouldn't have to wait for constructions to be finished to place further pending constructions.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req516</div>
</td>
<td>ELVES TRADING ANIMALS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Elves should be reluctant to trade animals to dwarves they haven't built up a relationship with.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req517</div>
</td>
<td>KIDNAP THOUGHT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Children should have some kind of thought about being kidnapped.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req518</div>
</td>
<td>OFFICE WORK
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Proper office work should require a table by the chair.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req519</div>
</td>
<td>BEAST INTERRUPTIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Beasts should not constantly interrupt workers that aren't actual under threat.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req520</div>
</td>
<td>EASY-TO-SKIP MEETING SCREENS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It's too easy to skip passed important meeting screens by pressing space.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req521</div>
</td>
<td>WATERY RAMPS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Rivers and pools need ramps for the most part, so you can move in and out of them. Seashores too.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req522</div>
</td>
<td>FLYING UP
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>In adventure mode, it needs to allocate sky for you if you try to fly up into unallocated areas.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req523</div>
</td>
<td>STREAM WATER FEET
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Stance points need to get water covers when walking over wet streams.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req524</div>
</td>
<td>SALT WATER PONDS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You can't designate salt water as a water source, so you can't make salt water ponds from the ocean for example.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req525</div>
</td>
<td>MORE RIVERS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Sometimes vast high-rain areas on the world map aren't drained, which is strange. The water systems could be underground, but there should be some in-game explanation.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req526</div>
</td>
<td>WOOD RAW TOKENS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Wood raws are missing a lot of customizable properties that other materials have.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req527</div>
</td>
<td>PROMPT MEETINGS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>People that have meetings should take care of them promptly. The broker should get to the trade depot when called.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req528</div>
</td>
<td>SOME NEW STOCKPILE SETTINGS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>People wanted stockpiles of meltables, stockpiles that exclude meltables, and stockpiles that distinguish dyed and undyed objects.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req529</div>
</td>
<td>CONSTRUCTION WEAR
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Constructions don't wear down in the same way that regular smoothed walls do.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req530</div>
</td>
<td>ORE/ALLOY AMOUNT PROBLEMS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Some of the alloy smelting reaction numbers don't match up with the amount of bar smelting reactions when they meet certain ores.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req531</div>
</td>
<td>IMPROVED WORLD MAP DURING EMBARK
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The tiles on the world map should be more representative of what's actually there.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req532</div>
</td>
<td>EDGE PROJECTILES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Ammunition fired at the edge of the play area shouldn't bounce against it.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req533</div>
</td>
<td>BUILDING CONSTRUCTION INCONSISTENCIES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are some issues about diagonal access and so on with building constructions.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req534</div>
</td>
<td>MINING SIDES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Dwarves sometimes go on a long detour to mine a wall from a particular side.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req535</div>
</td>
<td>WATER CONTAMINANT CONTROLS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Salinity and stagnance markers can spread up into areas they don't particularly belong, especially during adventure mode travel. Water shouldn't drop mud all the time.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req536</div>
</td>
<td>MAP BOULDER
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Should be able to pull up a stone object from boulder tiles.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req537</div>
</td>
<td>MASTER RECORD KEEPER
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Record keepers gain skill too quickly.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req538</div>
</td>
<td>MORE VEGETATION CONTROL
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Should be able to designation permanent zones for plant gathering and wood chopping and suspend all of these jobs as well.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req539</div>
</td>
<td>UNDIGNIFIED DIGGING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Somebody wanted to dig in the dirt without picks. It's not very dwarven, but I suppose it could be allowed.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req540</div>
</td>
<td>FULL REACTION CLASSES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Reaction classes need full definitions instead of just tokens like "FLUX" so that they can have more associated text and information.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req541</div>
</td>
<td>WATERPROOF AXLES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Somebody wanted to stick an axle through a wall without it leaking everywhere.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req542</div>
</td>
<td>NOBLE QUEUE ISSUES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The queue for meetings is updated sort of haphazardly. It could afford to be more organized.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req543</div>
</td>
<td>MEETING/CONVERSATION/GAME/PARTY LINKS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Many social activities in the game should be further organized and consolidated.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req544</div>
</td>
<td>WORK ANIMAL ASSIGNMENT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are various issues with work animal assignment. It needs to be updated.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req545</div>
</td>
<td>LAZY COMMANDERS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>In addition to whatever else is keeping soldiers from there stations and patrols, they should not hang out with their eating/sleeping/wounded commander.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req546</div>
</td>
<td>MORE FARM FAILURE INFORMATION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Farms need to explain more carefully what's going on when there's a biome or other conflict with available seeds. If you don't have any farmers, it should let you plant and fail before telling you.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req547</div>
</td>
<td>AUTOSAVE NAME CONFUSION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Autosaves that are loaded can generating somewhat confusing save names that have multiple seasons.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req548</div>
</td>
<td>IN-GAME INIT OPTION ACCESS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to change any reasonable init option within the game (flows as numbers, etc.).
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req549</div>
</td>
<td>PERSONALITY VS. PROFESSION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Some migrants come with personalities completely unsuited to their line of work. The personalities of dwarves you select for a given profession should matter more.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req550</div>
</td>
<td>CEILING INFORMATION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be given at least some information about the ceiling above a tile in certain circumstances.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req551</div>
</td>
<td>LOADED AREA IN OCEAN
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It keeps track of too many Z levels when you swim deep out into the ocean.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req552</div>
</td>
<td>STUCK AND CAN'T DIG
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Dwarves that get stranded in a pit can't dig out the floor, even if that's the best option.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req553</div>
</td>
<td>FLOWS VERSUS TREES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Water and magma should be able to flow passed (and burn for magma) trees.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req554</div>
</td>
<td>WALLS VERSUS MAGMA
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Constructed and non-constructed walls should be subject to magma. It can't just melt stone though, or the whole map will be affected (since magma currently generates its own heat).
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req555</div>
</td>
<td>MORE EFFECTIVE TRADE AGREEMENTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Traders should bring more of your high priority requests. In general, trade agreements can be more specific and interesting.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req556</div>
</td>
<td>SAVING SOUND OPTIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to save sound options back to the init file from the sound options menu.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req557</div>
</td>
<td>FROZEN RIVER SOURCES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>River source squares that are just going to stack water on ice squares shouldn't make water.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req558</div>
</td>
<td>MORE INFORMATION ABOUT ITEMS IN USE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Some sort of indication such as TSK for items that are in use from views other than the building item view with more information about what's going on available from the item's screen.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req559</div>
</td>
<td>POST-EMBARK LOAD INDICATOR
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>After getting through the final embark screen, it can leave you hanging for quite a while.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req560</div>
</td>
<td>WORKSHOP PROFILE CHANGE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>When setting workshop profile dwarves, it would be useful to be able to order the list in various ways (by relevant skills, unit type, whatever).
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req561</div>
</td>
<td>LOCAL WILDERNESS POPULATION REPLENISHMENT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Local wilderness populations don't replenish like they used to over the course of a dwarf mode game.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req562</div>
</td>
<td>ROOMS FOR APPOINTEES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Assigning rooms to appointees would help alleviate problems that come up after the mayor's election.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req563</div>
</td>
<td>CONSORT POSITION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Need to handle the position of consorts in the case that their spouses die.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req564</div>
</td>
<td>MORE CUSTOMIZATION FOR UNITS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You can set their type names, but being able to set colors or custom tiles should also be possible.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req565</div>
</td>
<td>CAGE CLEANING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You need to be able to free cages of all the various filth that collects there.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req566</div>
</td>
<td>SKY AND ICE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Need to make ice and sky look different, since there are conflicts at night.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req567</div>
</td>
<td>SMOOTHING AND ENGRAVING SKILLS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>They aren't closely related, so they should have different skills and professions, although having a skill entirely for smoothing stone seems like a stretch.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req568</div>
</td>
<td>LIMB BLOOD DROPPING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Bloody limbs that fly up in the air don't drop blood.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req569</div>
</td>
<td>BETTER TERRAIN ESCAPE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There is more that dwarves should be able to do while they are attempting to escape dangerous terrain, specifically in relation to running back and forth over squares they've already checked.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req570</div>
</td>
<td>VEGETATION TEMPERATURE TOLERANCE AND EMBARK READOUT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It needs to check temperature tolerances for local vegetation in embark, or else the tree/shrub density could be misleading.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req571</div>
</td>
<td>STANDING IS GOOD
</td>
<td>Completed
</td>
<td>If a passing cat or something forces a training marksdwarf to the ground, they'll stay on the ground throughout archery practice.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req572</div>
</td>
<td>PLEASE FORM A LINE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Activity zone users all stack up on the same square instead of looking normal and standing on square where they can move at full speed.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req573</div>
</td>
<td>BOOZE THE WOUNDED
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Wounded dwarves should be brought booze in the absence of or even instead of water.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req574</div>
</td>
<td>BUILDING MECHANISM LOSS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Triggered buildings that lose mechanisms (via melting etc.) shouldn't collapse, but it should let you know.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req575</div>
</td>
<td>UNDERWATER PROJECTILES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Throwing/shooting underwater are currently unrestricted, and projectiles in general should be handled.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req576</div>
</td>
<td>WANDERING FEATURE POPULATIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Creatures that reside in features and sites (such as subterranean creatures) should probably start and stay in their favored habitat, mostly.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req577</div>
</td>
<td>AMMO PRICES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>All prices have problems, but improved ammo is especially ridiculous.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req578</div>
</td>
<td>SAME SQUARE DRINKING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>If you are thirsty and it has been a while on dangerous semi-flooded terrain, you should go ahead and drink instead of being confused.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req579</div>
</td>
<td>CAGE TRAP CONTROLS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Need to have more control over cage trap loading to control spam. The load job should suspend if it can't find anything.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req580</div>
</td>
<td>FLUID TEMPERATURE TRANSFER
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It would be nice if warm water could transfer that temperature to squares that it moves into, for example, but it is potentially very expensive CPU-wise to force more temperature updates.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req581</div>
</td>
<td>CONTAMINANTS WITH CONTAMINANTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It needs to track whether you are covered with salt/filthy water for example.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req582</div>
</td>
<td>UPWARD DIAGONAL MOVEMENT RATES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It only applies a speed modifier to horizontal diagonal movement.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req583</div>
</td>
<td>REWALLING IN WATER
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to drop some simple constructions down into the water, as long as it's reasonable.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req584</div>
</td>
<td>MORE CONTROL OVER ROOMS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You shouldn't have to rely on the room-flow to get things right for you. There's also some issues with being able to set doors as 'internal' before they are built and whether or not constructions should be treated as walls before they are built.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req585</div>
</td>
<td>SNOWY WORLD GEN MAP
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The map in world generation is always covered with a lot of snow as it thinks it is the beginning of Spring.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req586</div>
</td>
<td>FORMER ENTITY MEMBER STATUS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Former entity members need to have that status tracked more explicitly.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req587</div>
</td>
<td>SUCCESSION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>New leaders need to come into power when the previous entity leader is lost. This happens automatically during world generation, but during play the process needs to be more prolonged most of the time.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req588</div>
</td>
<td>ADVENTURE MODE TOWN BED USE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Adventure mode townspeople don't think about beds properly and can often stack many, many people in a bed before they'd think about an available second bed.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Req589</div>
</td>
<td>CONSORT HANDLING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are some problems with how consorts are handled/finalized during world generation, and how that ties in to previous jobs that might have held.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr></tbody></table>

## Bloats

Bloats are somewhat superfluous ideas and thoughts of the Adams brothers.

<table>
<tbody><tr>
<th>Bloat-item
</th>
<th>Name
</th>
<th>Status
</th>
<th>Description
</th>
<th>Requirements
</th></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat1</div>
</td>
<td>ITEM GLOSSES
</td>
<td>Completed
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat2</div>
</td>
<td>MORE ITEM IMPROVEMENTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Do more with matching up specific item improvements to the item type. Figurines can have associated shapes, much like engravings, and these can generate more slots for improvement. An image added on an improvement can do the same thing. So a shield could have an image of a tortoise engraved on it, and the tortoise could be given ruby eyes, etc.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat3</div>
</td>
<td>PLAY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Kids should want toys and play with them, adults can use puzzle boxes.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat4</div>
</td>
<td>HIDDEN FUN STUFF
</td>
<td>Completed
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat5</div>
</td>
<td>BURROWS
</td>
<td>Completed
</td>
<td>(Upgraded) Moved this to a core item.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat6</div>
</td>
<td>BAR COUNTING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Keep track of bars and blocks more closely. It can count the number of bars and they could be thrown individual by the adventurer. This also allows the refinement of the amount of metal used by jobs.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat7</div>
</td>
<td>RENTAL UNITS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Allow bedrooms and dining rooms etc. to be rented out as units instead of the current bedroom only system.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat8</div>
</td>
<td>RENT SETTING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Improve the rent setting process. Could be through some kind of supply/demand calc, perhaps realized by hidden bidding.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat9</div>
</td>
<td>NOBLE RENT TWEAKS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Nobles tweaking the rent of places they like so as to obtain them.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat10</div>
</td>
<td>WHEELBARROWS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Wheelbarrows could be made and used by haulers to do several jobs at once. The AI for this is complicated though.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat11</div>
</td>
<td>FOOD HOARDERS BEWARE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Plagues of vermin for people that keep way too much food. Some of them could even be large-sized regular creatures. Fungus civilizations and large crawling beasts.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat12</div>
</td>
<td>VERMIN TROUBLE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>More vermin trouble for farmers in general. This makes vermin hunting pets more essential to have around. There can be various funny farm vermin. They could burrow in the farms. Creatures outside could also make burrows.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat13</div>
</td>
<td>HUNTING THE SMALL
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Ability to carry multiple small kills when hunting, hunters should also be able to hunt the smaller vermin that are running around without having to use a special job issued from a building.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat14</div>
</td>
<td>HUNTER AI
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Be smarter about returning kills when interrupted.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat15</div>
</td>
<td>FILTHY COMBAT EXTENSIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Throwing sand into eyes, spitting, spitting blood into eyes. Should be able to cup hands to pick up liquid/semi-liquid items so you can pitch vomit etc. at opponents.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat16</div>
</td>
<td>ADVENTURING IN DWARF MODE
</td>
<td>Completed
</td>
<td>(Obsolete -- there's no longer a notion of separate locations for these areas) In the same way as you'll send out baron patrols, send out patrols into wells and side dungeons (in the chasm etc.). Regular adventure mode could also go into these places.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat17</div>
</td>
<td>UNDERWATER DUNGEONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There should be undersea dungeons, swimming and diving. The liquid doesn't have to be water, especially in bad bad places. Ice could also be involved, and grottos open to air. Moats could use some of this. If the elves conquer your fortress they could turn it into a nature preserve of some kind.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat18</div>
</td>
<td>VERMIN AND DISEASE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Link vermin to plagues.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat19</div>
</td>
<td>FLUID WORKSHOPS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>More fluid multi-purpose workshops, where you can do simple tasks in a general purpose shop, but you can add tools to make more specialized objects with bonuses.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat20</div>
</td>
<td>TRAP CONVERSION ISSUES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It could change or add new traps to your dwarf cave when it is abandoned. It should only reset spent traps when it makes sense (ie the cave is inhabitated by intelligent creatures).
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat21</div>
</td>
<td>VARIOUS GHOST IDEAS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Blame the lever puller for flooded dungeon, make a ghost constantly pull the lever. Save relationships with various entities and have ghosts react to entity members as they would in life possibly. Ghosts can show you their bodies. Ghosts can pretend to do their old jobs. Ghost appearance can depend on how they died. Could force the adventurer to interact with ghosts to access now-secret parts of the fortress (could also use writings and legends for this). It can keep outposts that it normally throws away for fun little ghost stories. If the dwarves hate the elves, the ghosts could guide the adventurer to a poisoned goblet or insultingly engraved object to bring back to the elves, and they could lie to the player about it.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat22</div>
</td>
<td>ART HISTORY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Comments on art work, over different periods in the artist's career.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat23</div>
</td>
<td>END GAME EXPANSION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Extend adventure opportunities in end game caverns.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat24</div>
</td>
<td>ADVENTURE MODE TWEAK
</td>
<td>Completed
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat25</div>
</td>
<td>ELVEN FOREST RETREAT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Control elf forest retreat. They could be on break all the time.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat26</div>
</td>
<td>SIDE STORIES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Little scenarios and side stories added to caves to make them more interesting.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat27</div>
</td>
<td>ABSTRACT KNOWLEDGE SYSTEM
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Implement a more abstract "knowledge" system. Right now it tracks what items a civilization uses and what creatures it has seen, but there could be general things like how good their crossbow making knowledge is or even points of philosophy and law, etc. Knowledge could be transferred, lost and rediscovered.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat28</div>
</td>
<td>GATES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Sizeable giant gates that can be opened and closed remotely.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat29</div>
</td>
<td>MECHANISMS OUTDOORS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>

</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat30</div>
</td>
<td>AUTOMATE KITCHEN
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>

<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat31</div>

And they could see through it if they don't see anybody else around. Store owners should yell at you if you leave, but not send the whole town to kill you.

<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Ability to designate a metal vein for digging. They'd keep mining out the same kind of metal in adjacent squares as they uncover them.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat219</div>
</td>
<td>HISTORY GROUPING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>When you enter places, it could record it all of the subsequent events under a general heading which it names later, like "the raid of ".
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat220</div>
</td>
<td>AGING ITEMS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Items that should rot when you visit a site after some time will only undergo one change right now, they can go from fresh to being gone. It would be nice if item aging and rotting could be unified into a seamless system that let you age an item by any amount, but there are a number of obstacles.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat221</div>
</td>
<td>ART IMAGE COMPLEXITY AND IMPROVEMENT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Item improvement image complexity can be determined by craft quality, the image can generate slots for further improvements on the item to occupy, but it needs to do this in an abstract way that works for detail events as well, and also statues and figurines
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat222</div>
</td>
<td>FRIENDLY FIRE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>If you fire at an enemy 5 squares away and that creature is wrestling with a friendly, there should be a chance of hitting the friendly. But if the buddy is in the line of fire (by squares) but much closer to you, you shouldn't hit the buddy at all.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat223</div>
</td>
<td>WILDERNESS CAUTION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>

</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat224</div>
</td>
<td>STATUE IMPROVEMENTS
</td>

</td>
<td>
</td></tr>

</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat302</div>
</td>
<td>CREATURE ART ELEMENTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Allow art elements for abstract body parts, like eyes and hands, with tendencies toward the ones the artist's entity race actually has. Allow varying degrees of specificity for them.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat303</div>
</td>
<td>NOISE POLLUTION INDICATOR
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It might be helpful to have more of an idea where sleep-disturbing noise is coming from
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat304</div>
</td>
<td>UNDERGROUND TREE CAPS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Evaluate elf tree caps with respect to underground tree farms.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat305</div>
</td>
<td>GUILDS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Bring guild back more robustly as local and civ-wide organizations.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat306</div>
</td>
<td>HIDDEN FUN STUFF
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat307</div>
</td>
<td>DESCRIPTIVE INFO
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Ability to see genders information more easily in adventure mode. Ability to see other descriptive information as it comes in.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat308</div>
</td>
<td>QUALITY/DAMAGE CUSTOM STOCKPILES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Allow stockpiles to specify quality/damage state.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat309</div>
</td>
<td>SENSIBLE MIGRANT PROVISIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Immigrants should arrive with pack animals and various objects that make sense for their role and their journey.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat310</div>
</td>
<td>STRICT STONE BUILDING MATERIAL REQUIREMENTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Some buildings require rock blocks where metal ones would do.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat311</div>
</td>
<td>UNIT INVENTORY DISPLAY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Ability to see inventory somewhere on the unit profile screen.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat312</div>
</td>
<td>MIGRANT CONTROL
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Ability to refuse migrant groups with repercussions.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat313</div>
</td>
<td>STOCKPILE CONSOLIDATION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Ability to order the consolidation of items in a given stockpile. Part of the item stacking problem.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat314</div>
</td>
<td>DESIGNATION SIZE INFO
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>When selecting an area, for instance in mining designations, it could show you the dimensions and size of that area.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat315</div>
</td>
<td>DESIGNATION SHAPES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Option to designate with circles and custom templates.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat316</div>
</td>
<td>PIPE SECTIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Square-filling pipes sections that you can place and walk inside and hook together over many tiles and fill with fluids and stuff.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat317</div>
</td>
<td>PERSISTENT WILDS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Saving to some extent of non-site wilderness areas. The world is too large to save in its entirety, but some work can be done to save the places you've actually visited without completely filling your hard drive.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat318</div>
</td>
<td>VARIOUS GRASSES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Specific grass types associated by midmap square, rather than just 'grass'. Moss could also afford to occur in places other than old sites.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat319</div>
</td>
<td>IMPROVED BLOOD TRACKING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It can use stance point blood contaminants to handle blood tracking rather than spreading blood infinitely from spatters. In general, map blood amounts could afford to be tracked more closely.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat320</div>
</td>
<td>WORLD-SPANNING FEATURES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Use the world map feature system to create some world-spanning special map features, both indoor and outdoor, aside from rivers.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat321</div>
</td>
<td>FOOTPRINTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Footprints for walkers on sand, wet sand, snow, from blood contaminants on stance points, etc. Need some notion of foot print profiles by creature versus stance versus ground material.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat322</div>
</td>
<td>MISCELLANEOUS MATERIAL ACQUISITION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Finding jade and so on at certain rivers, finding pearls, amber, etc. Being able to obtain horn and ivory from creatures.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat323</div>
</td>
<td>WIDER USE OF COLOR DEFINITIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Tying all of the raws to color definitions would improve the coherence of the game. Minerals, plants, etc. should all be linked to color definition tokens.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat324</div>
</td>
<td>FOSSILS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Fossils beyond petrified wood. Another good way to start the random creature generator. Fossils could be collected by certain people.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat325</div>
</td>
<td>ADDITIONAL LIQUID TYPES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Candidates for new flowing "liquids" include sand, oil, mud, blood of various sorts, slime, farm products like grain and beer for the beer fountain. Only a limited number of materials from plant raws like beer would be able to be supported as a map flow at a given time.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat326</div>
</td>
<td>SKY AND TOPO DISPLAY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Add various optional methods for visualizing the 3D stuffs.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat327</div>
</td>
<td>VARIOUS RIDING POSITIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>When a creature rides another creature, it isn't very specific about where that creature is, and 'riding' is used for creatures carrying other creatures at times. The game would profit from more specific information.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat328</div>
</td>
<td>IMPROVED SEASHORES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Coastal biome designations, crabs, gulls, washed up jelly fish, ability to skillfully build sand castles in wet sand which can be worn down by waves, shells, washed up objects from distance civilizations that are on the body of water, seaweed, tidepools with anenomes, driftwood interactions.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat329</div>
</td>
<td>HIDDEN FUN STUFF
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat330</div>
</td>
<td>CLEANSING EVIL AREAS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The cleansing of evil areas needs to be put back in after tracking is improved.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat331</div>
</td>
<td>PRESSURE EQUILIBRIUM
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Liquid pressure equilibrium can be given a finer resolution at the cost of some map bits.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat332</div>
</td>
<td>PATTERN FLYING CRITTERS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Put the pattern flying cave feature critters back in.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat333</div>
</td>
<td>ARMOR CONTAMINATION FROM BLEEDING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Requires location specific bleeding information, tied to scarring and infection.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat334</div>
</td>
<td>IMPROVE LIQUID SINK DIRECTION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>River flows can be made to appear less diagonal on horizontal/vertical paths, but the routine is expensive.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat335</div>
</td>
<td>IMPROVED FARMING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Improved outdoor farming. I've already copied down a ton of NPK tables. We'll see what's inflicted upon you. A skilled farmer should make any fertilizer requirements very straightforward and easy to deal with.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat336</div>
</td>
<td>ITEM PLACEMENT VERSUS SMALL AREAS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Check some issues with the placement of objects versus small isolated areas.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat337</div>
</td>
<td>PEAT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Make peat more than just another soil type.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat338</div>
</td>
<td>MORE OUTDOOR MAP FEATURES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Various additional outside and inside features, like mesas, canyons, hill tops and so on.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat339</div>
</td>
<td>RIVER OVERFLOWS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>River overflows and the return of the associated farming techniques.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat340</div>
</td>
<td>BOULDER REMOVAL
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The ability to excavate boulders in the ground to make a stone object, as if you want another stone object.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat341</div>
</td>
<td>STUCK-IN CONTAMINANTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Issues with contaminants on item that becomes stuck inside another creature.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat342</div>
</td>
<td>MIGRANT HISTORICAL EVENTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Some issues with migrants and associated historical events.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat343</div>
</td>
<td>CLIMBING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Ability to climb terrain that would be otherwise impassable.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat344</div>
</td>
<td>RELIGIONS AND CULTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Return of things like the death and weapon nobles via potentially world-wide religions and cults.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat345</div>
</td>
<td>WEB MOVEMENT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Creatures that spin large webs should be able to move along the web, even in open space.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat346</div>
</td>
<td>SUPER NIGHT SKY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Stars, entity-named constellations with lines chosen for them. If there are hemispheres, visible stars can change with position. If the world rotates, they can move during the course of an evening.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat347</div>
</td>
<td>HEREDITY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Once there are enough physical and other candidate traits to be passed along, the game could model heredity/genetics with some simple algorithms and data.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat348</div>
</td>
<td>LINGUISTICS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Aside from having translations for basic words and a displayed written language, civ languages could also have a syntax, phonetic information, etc.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat349</div>
</td>
<td>UNIT NAMES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Right now it only lets you specify a few unit names. It should let you do more with profession names by race/entity. This ties in to having specialized unit names for certain customized professions within an entity.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat350</div>
</td>
<td>MORE MECHANISMS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Animal power for turning wheels etc., other pumps like tympanum wheels, various other things.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat351</div>
</td>
<td>UNAWARE OF PROFESSIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Your adventurer should not know somebody is a "guard" for example without knowing a bit about the civilization or at least doing some observation.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat352</div>
</td>
<td>RIVER/LAKE IMPROVEMENTS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There are various cosmetic improvements that can be made to under/above ground rivers and lakes.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat353</div>
</td>
<td>BARRACKS FUNCTION SPLIT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The barracks has various functions, and you should be able to say what goes on in a given barracks.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat354</div>
</td>
<td>SAVE KITCHEN SETTINGS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to save settings in the kitchen screen for later games. Applies to some other menus as well.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat355</div>
</td>
<td>TAKING NOTES
</td>
<td>Completed
</td>
<td>It would be nice to be able to take notes on both the embark screen world map and during the regular game that you can view later.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat356</div>
</td>
<td>IMPROVED FISHING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Dwarves should be able to fish for certain unit-sized creatures. Fish should be visible all over the place and dwarves should possibly have to catch them, depending on how the AI works out.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat357</div>
</td>
<td>HOLDING BREATH
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Being able to hold your breath in adventure mode might be an interesting mechanic. You wouldn't have to do it explicitly any time you wanted to swim, but it might be a neat way to interact with gases and other features.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat358</div>
</td>
<td>MACROS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to make keyboard macros to perform complicated repetitive actions.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat359</div>
</td>
<td>UNCONVENTIONAL AMMUNITION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to attempt to use various non-standard items in missile weapons and siege weapons.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat360</div>
</td>
<td>OTHER WATER SOURCES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>There should be various additional ways to obtain water. Rain, filling barrels from streams, simple desalination, etc.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat361</div>
</td>
<td>EMBARK PROFESSION UPDATE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>As you change skills on the embark screen, it should show the dwarf's resulting profession.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat362</div>
</td>
<td>VERMIN BODIES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Small animals should have full body representations, even if they don't have as many effects as they do for unit-sized creatures. These might only be allocated if something actually happens to the creature in question.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat363</div>
</td>
<td>FURNITURE FLUX
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to break down furniture and other objects that are made from materials that have occur in reactions (such as a flux) and use them at the smelter for example.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat364</div>
</td>
<td>SITE RECTANGLE INFORMATION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Sites should be able to give additional information about what is in their world map rectangle for map export and embark screen purposes.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat365</div>
</td>
<td>GLACIER/OCEAN INTERFACE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The boundary between the glacier and ocean biomes probably shouldn't be a regular beach in virtually all cases.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat366</div>
</td>
<td>SMOOTHER ELEVATION CHANGES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The game relies on cliffs too much to handle midmap elevation changes, especially in river valleys.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat367</div>
</td>
<td>WATER ON BUILDINGS DISPLAY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Water running over buildings currently blots them out. You should probably be able to see what's there -- grates and bars at the very least.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat368</div>
</td>
<td>GENDER-BASED PICTURES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You should be able to further specify which graphical tiles are used if you like.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat369</div>
</td>
<td>LEGENDARY SWIM SPEED
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>It could afford to make finer gradations for swim skill passed legendary.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat370</div>
</td>
<td>ITEMS AND GRATES/BARS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Small items should have a chance of falling through floor grates. Floor bars should be even worse. Projectiles should also interact with grates and bars (similar to fortifications).
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat371</div>
</td>
<td>SUBORDINATE SITE POPULATIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>A site that becomes subordinated under a dwarf site should still be able to maintain its inhabitants after the dwarf game.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat372</div>
</td>
<td>MORE WITH TRAPS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Traps, being far too powerful, should probably have to be set up. For instance, you should be required to actually create a stonefall trap using a few different locations to get it to work. Pulling things apart like this will also allow more versatility.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat373</div>
</td>
<td>IMPROVED DRUNKS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Adventure mode drunks shouldn't be a separate unit type. They are currently determined by the immoderation personality facet, but much more can be done here. In general, there's an implication that world gen personalities should be stored for more units, but that is expensive memory-wise.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat374</div>
</td>
<td>CONVERSATION SKILLS IN ADVENTURE MODE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>With the proper skills, you should be able to judge the mood of people you are talking to in finer and finer degrees. You should also be able to exercise the various conversation skills that can be used in dwarf mode.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat375</div>
</td>
<td>PRIVACY AND ADVENTURE MODE CONVERSATIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Depending on personality scores, people you come up to on the street might not want to talk to you about personal matters when they don't know you.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat376</div>
</td>
<td>TALKING TO GODS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Instead of being forced to join a temple first, a player should be able to attempt to speak to any known deity.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat377</div>
</td>
<td>TEMPLE-SPECIFIC TASKS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>High priests at the temple should have various things for you to do. Though it's best to have all task assignments occur within a general framework according to the goals of the task-giver, just being the leader of the temple and a strident believer in a deity will no doubt give rise to interesting and specific quests. Building a new temple and spreading the faith? Recovering holy relics? Dealing with rivals? Objectives could be vague to remove some of the quest straitjacket effect that can develop, as long as it has means to track your progress and you have various means to accomplish the task at hand. It would be cool to be accepted as a temple higher-up once you manager to spread the faith into an outlying village.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat378</div>
</td>
<td>MORE BUILDING CONVERSATION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Right now, you only hear architecture comments when there's a direct relationship between the building and a deity, and those relationships are fairly rare. It could talk about the architect, the central figure around which the building was designed, any of the elements used in the architecture, the material used and its relationship any underlying concepts, etc.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat379</div>
</td>
<td>LEGEND SUBSET SELECTION
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The legends screen is virtually useless once enough objects and events have been discovered. You should be able to select subsets in various ways, whether by creature type or building type or time period or event type or even using a map to go by geographic area.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat380</div>
</td>
<td>MOODS AND IMPRESSIONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>In both modes, relationships need to be stored with some more information. People should have short and long-term impressions of the people that they meet, at least as many as it can safely store (and always with the player). They can also have short and long-term moods that are altered by their interactions with others.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat381</div>
</td>
<td>GENERALIZE ARCHITECTURE SYSTEM
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The system used for temples linking abstract concepts to architectural elements should be put in to place for other structures, especially castles and towers.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat382</div>
</td>
<td>MORE SPHERE LINKS FOR TEMPLES
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Right now, only certain deity spheres get architecture links in their corresponding temples. There's more that can be done there, and the concept can be expanded from architecture to many other elements of the faith/temple, so that certain type of items/creatures/festivals/rituals/etc. occur in the temple.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>Bloat383</div>
</td>
<td>GOBLIN DUNGEONS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The goblins lost the tunnel network under their towers with the building revision, and they want it back in improved form with various untoward goings on.
</td>
<td>
</td></tr></tbody></table>

## Power Goals

Power Goals are the ultimate missions for the future.

<th>Status
</th>

<td>ARTIFACT QUEST
</td>

</td></tr>

<td>THE SYSTEM
</td>

</td>
<td>You are apprehended, brought to a jail, chained to the wall, interrogated and beaten, brought before the ruler, sentenced, put on public display locked in stocks, subjected to thrown vegetables and ridicule, brought up to a platform and executed.

<td><div>PowerGoal 15</div>

<td>REVENGE AND THE WILDERNESS

<td><div>PowerGoal 16</div>

<td>THERE ARE WOMEN UPRIVER!
</td>

</td>

</td>

</td>

<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 58</div>
</td>
<td>WHAT WERE WE THINKING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You are sent to rescue the prince and princess, who have been captured by the evil wizard. You uncover the sinister and disgusting plot to breed the siblings to form an evil army of mutants.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 59</div>
</td>
<td>FOOLHARDY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The warriors line up behind the row of rocks with their crossbows as the goblins charge on their beakdogs. You jump up and charge the goblins in turn. The warriors shout, "You're a fool! Don't do it!". Right as the first approaches you, you duck and swing, slicing off the beakdog's legs.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 60</div>
</td>
<td>HUNGRY LIKE THE WOLF
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You put on a talisman, turn into a werewolf, and your buddy screams, "take it off!", but you can't understand him with your beast mind and soon fill with rage and kill him.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 61</div>
</td>
<td>REVERSE COMPOUND FRACTURE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>As you stumble through the tomb, you bump into an ogre chewing on a corpse with a leg bone still in its mouth. The beast bites the bone in half and stabs you in the throat with the jagged edge, gouging it out.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 62</div>
</td>
<td>THE EVIL PLAYER MEETS THE GOBLINS
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You kidnap a child and take it to the goblins. They let you in and seem interested in buying it, but you struggle to speak with them. After a protracted negotiation involving displaying objects and gestures, you seal the deal.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 63</div>
</td>
<td>INEXPERTLY AND REPEATEDLY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You swing at the goblin, overextending. It kicks you in the stomach and your feet slip out from under you. When you hit the ground, you drop your sword. The goblin holds its giant club over its head, but with the weight of the club, it loses its balance and stumbles. You leap forward up from the ground and rush the goblin but trip on a rock and land in some serpent vines, which entangle you. You struggle to free yourself, but aren't in time as the goblin inexpertly and repeatedly clubs you until you die.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 64</div>
</td>
<td>THE LOCAL SPIRIT
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You take a coin that had been offered at the village shrine. A red deer runs across a nearby field, pausing to stare directly at you for a heartbeat, and then disappears beyond the woodline. You turn into a kobold that night. Your best friend feels compelled to kill you to put you out of your misery.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 65</div>
</td>
<td>KINDA LIKE SNOW WHITE OR SOMETHING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You are sent to steal another potion from the elves. The beautiful queen is appearing before her lustful and incompetent generals the following day and she must remove the marks of her advancing age.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 66</div>
</td>
<td>IT WAS WORTH THE TIME
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The guards are hunting you because you have a stolen diamond. You dip into a group of mourners that are a viewing a body just as the guards appear. They dare not stop you from slipping into the solemn crowd. You pretend to mourn the body, holding its head in your hand as you hide the diamond in the corpse's mouth. Although you are subsequently apprehended and imprisoned for twenty years, after you are released, you dig up the body and claim the prize.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 67</div>
</td>
<td>THE LOCAL ARENA
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You are in charge of taking the bets for the local arena. Somebody loses all their farm animals on a fight, and you have to collect the debt.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 68</div>
</td>
<td>MAGICAL IDIOT PEEPER
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Hearing of her exotic beauty, the young knave uses a ring to open a portal in the wall of the princess's bedroom, wrenching a small cylinder of stone out of the wall via its magical powers. However, the stories had all been told by Southmen, and she was in fact quite unattractive by the different standards of his culture.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 69</div>
</td>
<td>THE LEGEND OF THE HORAK PART I
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The two princes, friends from childhood, laughed as they rode their horses. They got off and tied them to a tree. Then a giant Horak stepped out of the woods. The princes tried to keep it back with their telescoping knife-poles, but it charged forward and swallowed them whole.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 70</div>
</td>
<td>MUDSLIDE
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You summon the tribal elders to the top of the slope. An army has gathered and is chanting prior to their charge up against your position. The elders perform a dance, it rains heavily, and the resulting mudslide buries the enemy soldiers alive. They all slowly die.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 71</div>
</td>
<td>YOUR PLATFORM
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The slaves carry a giant platform on which you sit above the reserves behind your advancing army. When your army enter a narrow ravine, you are fired upon by kobold archers.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 72</div>
</td>
<td>YEAH!
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The explorers make their way down the cave river, past dangerous stalagmites in the rapids, turn to face each other and do a high-five.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 73</div>
</td>
<td>WHERE'S MY MONEY?
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The money changer shorts you a gold piece, so you declare a quest for vengeance. You and your fellow adventurer sneak into his house three days later. You hold a jagged knife to his neck, and he gasps. You lower your dagger to his coin purse, cut it loose, then take the coin he owes you.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 74</div>
</td>
<td>A PAINFUL MEMORY
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>On the toll bridge, you see a man with two locks of hair curled around his ears and the rest of his head shaved. He wields a great club. You twitch and hold your knee where you've been wounded by a club before. As you go to pay the toll, your nervousness causes you to stutter, and he looks at you suspiciously.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 75</div>
</td>
<td>SONG OF DREAD
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The evil spirit of the wood caused the people's hair to turn white. On the day of the sacrifice, the priest sang a song of such dread that many villagers volunteered to die just to free themselves from the memory of its singing.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 76</div>
</td>
<td>THE GENERAL'S RING
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You go to the refugees, and they tell you that the general of the invading army leaves his signet ring in his dresser drawer before he goes to bed. You steal it that night.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 77</div>
</td>
<td>YUCK!
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You lock blades with Snylar the Lizardman. During the contest of strength, you tell it that it is little more than a hatchling. It tells you that your beard is patchy and sparse. You spit a glob of saliva into his eye. He sprays spit at you in return.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 78</div>
</td>
<td>WEREWOLF HUNTER
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>Dolnar the hunter teaches you how to track the werewolf by its sign and by its call. She recommends a special breed of hunting dog, known for its ability to track down and contest the elusive beasts.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 79</div>
</td>
<td>THE LEGEND OF THE HORAK PART II
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>The tribesman have passed down for generations the story that the giant Horak cannot be harmed by blades, so the mighty barbarian traveling with you decides to wrestle the beast and tears it apart with his bare hands.

<tr>
<td><div>PowerGoal 80</div>
</td>
<td>BUT HOW DID HE DIE?
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>
<td>You ask the peasant how the mayor was murdered, and you are told in detail how the mayor was stabbed to death and toppled out of the window landing two stories below.

<td><div>PowerGoal 81</div>

<td>JOURNEY TO THE UNDERWORLD
</td>
<td>(Future)
</td>

You try to do the same and bump into the wall, hurting your nose, as mortals are not admitted without an escort. He pokes his head back out and smirks, and then grabs your hand and pulls you through.
</td></tr>

<td><div>PowerGoal 82</div>

<td>ON KARNAK'S GOOD SIDE
</td>

</td>

</td></tr>

<td><div>PowerGoal 83</div>

<td>DENIED ENTRY - THE PROPHECY!

</td>

</td>

You can see the cold breath of people far away as the witch's servants leave the haunted cemetery.
</td></tr>

<td><div>PowerGoal 85</div>
</td>
<td>RETIRE AS A PEASANT

</td></tr>

<td>DO NOT MOVE FROM THAT SPOT

</td>

Your sons come up and say that they are his brothers. Shangris holds a knife to your neck, screaming that he has no father. Your sons step back. The confusion disorients the crowd, allowing your evil son Shangris to escape.

</td></tr>

While the official is off buying fine whiskey and an expensive candelabrum, you find that you are still afflicted.

The elf says the Charm of the Trees and the tree they are hiding under engulfs the knight, putting him into stasis.
